## C2 — ym_history_part2.patch (b33d58d 10-29 01:24 .. 264398b 05:26), 6,362/6,362 lines, digest cross/digest_C2.md (about 115 KB)
Where it crossed or came near:
1. The hierarchical (Migdal–Kadanoff) model is certified across the crossover. In 48d055e (02:22) §8.3, rev 9, the MK flow from β = 2.3 falls below c₁ ≤ 0.131 at step 7, with 256-bit directed rounding. The paper said: "On that model the chain closes in a handful of steps from the crossover coupling." MassGapChain.lean (1a7e2b0, 04:10, compiled at 04:36 with no sorry) has this in a comment only: "the hierarchical model's … closed at k = 8". The record behind it is in the III tree: tilt_cert.2026-10-29b.txt (file time 01:03, committed 7ff34274e at 04:55): the tilt budget closes at k = 8, B ∈ [0.0335, 0.0340] from 2.2 and [0.2815, 0.2856] from 2.3. This does not transfer to ℤ⁴ without using the group: Ito's theorem, cited on the D2 page, makes the MK flow reach strong coupling for U(1) too.
2. A four-dimensional reading at β = 2.2 (cd1082a, 04:33): on the exact 2⁴ cube with spins ≤ 1, the opposite-face coupling is 0.010741, 4% of the one-link value; refuted the stake "of order one". Companion 264398b (05:26): a frustrated datum moves the face mean by 0.649, so "the worst-case form of the quotient criterion is dead at 2.2 in this model."
3. The proved strong end: from 0.1309 to Theorem 8.7 (af03ccf, 03:25), β ≤ 0.241, or 0.2452 with the exact constants. "reaches no crossover" is accurate.
4. The tube result is 2+1-dimensional; the β₃ = 2.3 spectra are readings, not the 4D crossover.
5. The missing link, last stated: in 59a1f94 and b18d301 the worst-case contraction grows about 2× per doubling at β = 0.3 and 0.5, while the averaged response contracts: "the missing theorem is the one that makes an averaged contraction imply decay."
The retreat: Rev 10 (9d5db99, 02:35) deleted the model sentence above; it became "certifies the entry into the disk from its fifth rung and the threshold at its seventh, and not the crossover's first steps", on the same record: the first step goes 2.2 → 2.34 against one-loop ℤ⁴ 2.2 → 1.94. Rev 11 (d9d6527, 02:39) added "no step count of the hierarchical model is a reading of it", with no new evidence.
Hedges: H1 rev 10 a partial deflation; H2 rev 11 a relabel without evidence; H6 the opposite, an inflation: D2 §5(ii) (dd54269) says "V.1's n₀ is our K*" and drops §2's own condition "if the b's agree". The other changes are corrections backed by evidence.
Two facts: the quoted sentence is in no landed text (look in session replies); the §3b Monte Carlo stake for one blocking at β = 2.3 was withdrawn unrun by the operator's own order (cd1082a); the β = 2.3 boundary-response stake S1 was left untested (sampler noise floor), not refuted (ea7ffa3).
The single missing step (creative-solve): a group-dependent, measure-weighted one-blocking theorem on ℤ⁴ for a 2⁴ cell at β ≈ 2.2–2.3, in place of the worst-case form; first step: compute the weight of the frustrated data on the exact cube, with U(1) at β = 1.2 as the control.

## C6b — 25 Lean files (lean_part2.list), 6,422 lines, 394,581 bytes, digest cross/digest_C6b.md
Strongest crossing in the kernel of record: Unitary.lean:237 `wilson_lattice_potential_gap_unitary`: the static potential is s > 0 at every β > 0, β = 2.3 included; any in-plane β′ and every finite group with a faithful unitary representation (SU(2)'s finite subgroups among them), on a slab of finite cross-section; built by lean_arm 36 (lake build, 22 theorems) in kernel/runs/aperanton_gate.2026-10-27u, GATE_RC=0; all 18 kernel files of the list built in that run. Crosses in coupling, not in object: G finite, cross-section finite, no bound uniform in the cross-section. Wilson.lean:114 the same at β ≥ 0 given W(1) > 0. Theorems valid at every coupling: XTransfer:552, Spectral:197/233 (σ ≥ 0 exists), StrongCoupling:159/178 (W ≥ y^{RT}), Weak:167/207, Torus4Bridge:232, YM2:93/181 (2D, exact).
Hierarchical model: in the Lean, prose only (MassGapChain.lean:19 "closed at k = 8"). Certificates outside Lean in gawk at 256 bits: the mkflow records (K* = 7 to c₁ ≤ 0.131; c₁ = 0.0086 at k = 8); III's arm 120: the budget OPEN at K* and CLOSING at K* + 1 for β = 2.2, 2.3 and 5; arm 120 printed in III's run 2026-11-01d, which is RED because a tooth survived on arm 114.
The 0.131 threshold: 0.131 = 3π/72, the coupling at which the Dobrushin row sum 18·4β/(3π) reaches 1, the hypothesis of cov_le; no Lean file computes it. The record compares c₁ = I₂/I₁ (about β/4 at small β) against a coupling threshold: the units differ. Arm 120's OPEN at step 7 and CLOSING at step 8 is exactly what the coupling reading predicts, so "k = 8" stands.
The crossover as a hypothesis: the drafts prove the chain conditional on CrossoverBudget (MassGapChain:36/47/63/73, UVInterface:47, MassGapAssembly:108/119/137, CrossoverCriterion:455/506); none instantiates it at any β; none of the drafts is in the run of record — they compiled only in III's runs al, 11-01b and 11-01d, all RED. III-Yang_Mills' DobrushinMeasure is the uncompiled 5-error text; III's 1172-line copy differs in three proofs, statements unchanged; MassGapChain differs by one line; the 18 kernel files byte-identical to III's HEAD and to commit 947aca7c.
Other: Z2Chain:85 clauses_part proves the Z₂ clauses (β < 0.0716 and β > 1.445) leave the transition at 0.4407 uncovered; Z2Inversion:403 and Z2Moment:166–192 decided at y = 1/4 (β ≈ 0.69), identities only.
The single missing theorem: an instance of CrossoverBudget for the blocked SU(2) measure at β ≈ 2.2–2.3, over Haar measure, uniform in the cutoff.

## C3 — ym_history_part3.patch (a461a1a 10-29 05:27 .. ecbc2c2 22:07), 4,724/4,724 lines, 140 entries + synthesis A–F, digest cross/digest_C3.md
Strongest crossings at their own strength:
1. CERTIFIED, hierarchical model not ℤ⁴ (C3-014): §8.3 unchanged rev 19–25 and in today's paper: the MK flow from β = 2.3 to below c₁ = 0.131 in 7 steps (18 from 5, 37 from 10); records mkflow_*.2026-10-29d, pentapylon_gate 2026-10-29w GREEN 02:20.
2. Exact readings on a 4D 2×2 square: spin-1 model 2.2 → 1.354 → 0.749 → 0.168 (1f3e161, 06:24), withdrawn 06:42 (b67cddf) only "as a reading of SU(2)" on a measured truncation defect; spins ≤ 2 (665f630, 07:46): 2.2 → 1.888 → 1.553 → 1.161 → 0.662 → 0.109, below 0.2452 at step 5; from 3.0, step 8; the last steps the strong-coupling area law (F1). Limits: boundary fixed (ordered); β_eff by matching the 2×2 loop to one plaquette, not Hypothesis I's coupling; a U(1) control shows a +0.26 bias at this boundary.
3. 3D chain with both ends proved in print (2cf337c, cb65649): Theorem 8.8 at β ≤ 0.3739 plus Bałaban's unconditional 1985 theorem; about 6 renormalisation steps uncovered out of 7.3–7.8.
4. 4D reduction and the UV half: Theorems 7.4 and 8.5 reduce the gap to Hypotheses I/II; distance estimated at about 260 + 2–4 steps (e589934); Bałaban's Theorem 2 reduced to Lemmas B and C (c9b987f); a bounded transient suffices (249d913), CouplingTransient.lean compiled.
5. Flux-surface gas: the singly-attached sub-gas reaches β* = 0.4759 with every input proved; the full gas waits on one finite table θ (ecbc2c2), precursor of Theorem 8.9.
Changes: every withdrawal in this window rests on a measured or read defect: the 0.168 flow; "the hard half collapses to one number" (630a783); the "γ = 6, proved" threshold, withdrawn 16:35 (074c5b4); the s_max paragraph (5bf8ae8). Scope widened twice: Bałaban's Theorem 2 became this work's own theorem to prove (a49fe0b, 06:14); an unsourced dismissal of Magnen–Rivasseau–Sénéor struck (d4bea41, 06:56).
Hedges: one conditional hedge, "no reading of the hierarchical model is a statement about ℤ⁴": accurate for Hypothesis I, understates only relative to the centre-vortex route where bond-moving bounds feed Tomboulis's construction (F2). By omission: the README and abstract never say the certified descent starts at β = 2.3.
Over-statements: the 30267c8 header "β* = 0.4260, every input proved"; "Never appeared" for Bałaban's Theorem 2 on the zbMATH listing only; §90 "what is proved in between is the running itself".
Single missing step: on the paper's route, a measure-weighted renormalisation step across the 2–4 step strong bridge; on the centre route, a group-dependent matching inequality (the step Ito–Seiler found unproved outside strong coupling) which would make K* = 7 a ℤ⁴ statement. 3D is the nearest complete crossing.

## C5c — iii_history_part3.patch (93802cf3c 10-30 06:25 .. 6bc35b0e7 11-01 20:37, 77 commits), 2,457/2,457 lines, digest cross/digest_C5c.md
Holds the corrections, not the original claims; touches S1, the crossing campaign, the far shore, THE STANDING (new), PENTAPYLON (one appended signature), four ΤΥΡΒΗ Navier–Stokes Lean files. None of: an ΑΠΕΡΑΝΤΟΝ master-plan page, a hierarchical-model Lean file, "V.1's n₀ is our K*", "closed at k = 8", "does not cross—or claim to cross".
Nothing here proves anything at β ≥ 0.5 for SU(2) on ℤ⁴, and nothing claims to. Crossings that exist:
- The MK descent (far shore, ce081ee78): K* = 7 from β = 2.2/2.3 to c₁ ≤ 0.131, certified at 256 bits on the hierarchical model; refuted as ℤ⁴'s zeroth layer with a mechanism: the first rung rises, and e72d89fa6 shows the model's multi-plaquette content zero by construction ("L4 is VACUOUS"); its tilt budget reads "CLOSING at k = K* + 2 = 9 … STAKED" against MassGapChain's "closed at k = 8".
- Exact-object readings at β = 2.2: the blocked action's multi-plaquette (non-Wilson) content contracts ×0.10–0.34 per blocking step; Hypothesis I's own coefficient summed over adjacent pairs at two steps is 0.53–0.60 at spins ≤ 1 (nearest approach, but a lower bound with the spin cutoff not converged), 1.11–1.33 at spins ≤ 3/2; the staked ladder fell for SU(2) (×0.55, ×0.36) and rose for U(1) at 1.2, withdrawn at 12:21 when U(1) fell ×0.90 at the cancelling datum and fired the preregistered clause.
- The tube spectrum at 2.3: 2+1-D, spin-truncated, measured.
Nearest chain: the far shore §10 → RESULTS 4 reduces the crossing to one run of the existing certificate at the blocked coefficients; THE STANDING lists this reduction as proved; its exit budget certified but uncommitted and not pinned by a gate run. THE STANDING: Theorem 8.5 proved; (E), H-I, H-II open. At 02:1x the far shore's table listed H-II as proved; corrected at 20:3x to open in the uniform form ("one missing link and three").
Theorem 8.9's day (each change names a real gap): 01:30 unconditional 0.4764; 02:33 conditional on S12; 15:25 a "floor" 0.4672 "on proved lemmas alone"; 18:48 the floor conditional too; the one unconditional number 0.2452.
Hedges: (h1) "Nothing above 0.2452 is claimed" beside a certified-conditional 0.3956; (h2, marginal) "MEASURED" on the certified descent; (h3) THE STANDING §9 itself: the "not claimed" lists "stated the proved reduction only by omission" — matches the operator's complaint; (h4) THE STANDING omits the descent and the tube except as refuted roads. Over-statement left uncorrected: "proved reach 0.4709" after J6's withdrawal. At 18:59 the negative claim "hypothesis not met at 4a from 2.2" withdrawn (keeps the crossing open).
Creative-solve: a measure-weighted decay lemma — the chessboard weight of frustrated outsides; first experiment already staked in the far shore §7.

## C6a — 27 Lean files (lean_part1.list), 7,497 lines, digest cross/digest_C6a.md
Verdict: none of the 27 proves an SU(2) mass gap uniform in volume at any β ≥ 0.5. Nothing narrowed after the 2026-10-27u run of record: all 27 match its MANIFEST blobs, 26 byte-identical to III HEAD (the root file differs only by 22 imports added later). The narrowing that happened is header prose: GapBound's first landing (90e9bbed7) titled "THE STRING TENSION IS A GAP"; fifty minutes later (ca608fe9f) the header renamed the quantity the static potential at fixed T and disclaimed the area law; git diff confirms no theorem line moved.
Holds at β = 2.3 at its scope (kernel): Separation.lean:312 wilson_lattice_potential_gap_faithful (strongest): every finite group with a faithful Wilson action, every β > 0, every finite cross-section, no extra hypothesis: the Wilson loop converges and its static potential is strictly positive, at least that sector's gap. Also MassGap:22; GapBound:348 string_tension_z2 (Z₂, no hypotheses); Nonabelian:367; GaugeAction:176. Price (MassGap's header): no lower bound uniform in the cross-section. Infinite volume, Z₂, every coupling, existence only: Lattice:293 Winf_tendsto, Rectangle:440 string_tension_exists (σ ≥ 0 on ℤ³).
The volume-uniform engine is conditional: Dobrushin:751 cov_le, Slab:624 transfer_gap_uniform, Rowsum:278, Decay:50 — row sums ≤ α < 1 over a finite spin type; discharged only in prose, for Z₂ at β < 0.1257 (d=3), 0.0716 (d=4); no decide or certificate at any β ≥ 0.5.
Nearest in kind, Z₂ only: self-duality (Duality:125, wegner_dual :261) exact at every β sends β = 2.3 to 0.01005, inside the certified region. III's TorusT3Assembly.lean (in neither list) builds a Z₂ weak-coupling clause at β > 1.445; its gate runs 10-31an and 11-01d RED.
The 0.131 threshold: in no Lean file; the paper's SU(2) one-link threshold in β (content4.js:12: 3π/72 = 0.1309, a non-Lean enclosure); mkflow.awk applies the same number to c₁ = I₂/I₁; c₁ = 0.131 corresponds to β = 0.530. From β = 2.3 the flow reaches c₁ = 0.0832 at k = 7 and 0.0086 at k = 8, below c₁(0.1309) = 0.0327 — the hierarchical model only.
Missing theorem: a certified α < 1 for the true blocked measure, written as a FactorSystem, at one physical scale reached from β ≈ 2.3 (Dobrushin.lean already allows block sites), plus the lift to a compact group.
Coverage 27/27 files, 7,497 lines; no code-level sorry, axiom or native_decide; none a draft. III's run 11-01e reports "Lattice.lean REFUSED" on identical bytes, not reproduced.

## C1 — ym_history_part1.patch (7cabe20 10-28 21:56 .. 5f2c1ba 10-29 01:06), 15,819/15,819 lines (49 chunks), digest cross/digest_C1.md
Correction to carry: 0.131 is the one-link Dobrushin limit on β: β < 3π/72 = 0.13090 (III PENTAPYLON RESULTS 14.1); mkflow compares the character coefficient c₁ against that number instead. RESULTS 15.1 on the same page gives the right threshold for the flowed weight, c₁ < 0.0327, "entered one step past K*"; the e-records reach it at K*+1 = 8/8/19/38 (β = 2.2/2.3/5/10), matching MassGapChain "closed at k = 8". Check what c₁ ≤ 0.131 denotes in Dobrushin.lean before stating K* = 7.
Strongest crossing: commit 5f2c1ba certifies the hierarchical (Migdal–Kadanoff) descent from β = 2.2 and 2.3 into strong coupling: 256-bit directed rounding, priced tails, a known answer contained and a deliberate wrong-rounding version refused; holds only on the hierarchical lattice, where the recursion is exact. mkflow.awk's header credits the theorem as classical (Ito 1985; Mueller–Schiemann); the certificate is the tree's. The MANIFEST line "On the hierarchical model, not on ℤ⁴" is an exact label, not a hedge.
Nearest on ℤ⁴: revision 6 proves the reduction (Theorems 8.4/8.6); revision 7 (b33d58d) corrected "a single statement" to two hypotheses (II + III), uniform in the cutoff; the consumer CrossoverCriterion.lean is a draft outside the kernel record; the latest aperanton gate run RED. The one missing step: a one-sided, SU(2)-specific inequality bounding ℤ⁴'s blocked influence by the certified hierarchical flow; it must depend on the group (revision 9's U(1) no-go); it must be a real bound, not a small correction (the Far Shore measured the hierarchical step's error at rung 1 as large as the step itself); the record's numbers point the right way: the hierarchical flow needs eight doublings from 2.3, ℤ⁴ two or three.
Hedges: the 5f2c1ba MANIFEST and the revision 6 README (P:986) keep the descent out of the paper by gate policy; the kernel MANIFEST (P:1268) keeps CrossoverCriterion and MassGapChain out of the kernel record by the same policy; revision 10 (9d5db99, 10-29 02:35) deleted a true sentence: "On that model the chain closes in a handful of steps from the crossover coupling"; the README still says the paper "does not yet cite" the descent (cited since revision 9, 48d055e).
Overstated: "the exact spectra of the SU(2) tube" (the record truncated at spin 1, double precision, 2+1D), and "threshold at the seventh step" (the correction above).
Not a hedge: S1:95's "no method … certifies one" (about ℤ⁴ steps) is accurate; part 1's prose gets sharper from revision 1 to 7, not weaker.
The operator's sentence: in none of the 192 commits; nearest: revision 13 (af03ccf) "it reaches no crossover" (README; content4.js:146 "claims nothing above β = 0.2452"); S1:95 "It does not cross it."
Loose ends: two helper files (c1_chunks.txt, c1_tmp_patchfiles.txt); the patch omits 5f2c1ba's eight instruments (mkflow.awk read whole, the gate script grepped, the .pl files not read); a read-only git log -S (task bqo289dls) still running.

Seats: b10e8d4b = ΑΠΕΡΑΝΤΟΝ ("infinite-mass-gap-4d-plan"), c3419161 = iii-38, 59ca2c52 = iii-43 (orchestrator), cd9abb9e = iii-3a.

CERTIFIED on the Migdal–Kadanoff (hierarchical) model: iii-38's 256-bit flow reached the threshold at K* = 7 from β = 2.2 and 2.3 (10-29T04:12Z); b10e8d4b's interval-certified tilt budget then entered the SU(2) single-link Dobrushin disk at k = 8: B₈ ∈ [0.28151, 0.28192] from 2.3 (04:57Z). MassGapChain.lean (04:45Z) takes Σ1 and Σ2 as hypotheses and proves nothing about them; its header names the hierarchical instance "closed at k = 8". iii-3a read the descent as the hypothesis of Tomboulis's V.1 ("K* = 7 IS the n₀", if the decimation parameter b matches) and found V.1's derivation broken (08:43Z).

MEASURED on a ℤ⁴ object, then withdrawn: on a 2×2 square, β = 2.2 ran 1.354 → 0.749 → 0.168 at the ordered datum in a spin-1 truncated model, into the proved region in three steps (iii-38 09:56–10:14Z); corrected within the hour: with a random datum both groups collapse (10:14Z), at spins ≤ 2 the one-step ratio 0.86 (10:42Z).
3. CONDITIONAL: THE GROUND's Theorem 8.5 (Hypotheses I + II imply the gap); a pasted 10-28 "continuum lift, conditional on H".
4. STATED, then contradicted by measurements: b10e8d4b 10-28T09:04Z: the window [0.131, 2.3] "is a cost problem, not a physics problem … the block criterion is easily true."
The scale-back: the hierarchical gap withdrawn as prior art (Ito 1985, Müller–Schiemann; 10-29 00:38Z); iii-43 restricted every record to "on the hierarchical model … not a statement about ℤ⁴" (04:50Z); the U(1) falsifier: any transfer that ignores the gauge group would also give 4D U(1) a gap (05:50Z); iii-38's RESULTS 16.5 showed the MK flow moving the wrong way at its first rung (06:32Z), revisions 10–11 followed; ONE SCALE-BACK WENT FURTHER THAN ITS EVIDENCE: iii-43's "the proof that it reads nothing about ℤ⁴" (08:25Z); "It does not cross the bridge" (14:07Z); on 11-02 00:44Z iii-43 admitted applying one "not claimed / no verdict" register "uniformly to proven, implied and open material alike." The exact sentence the operator quoted appears in no transcript before his message and in no commit.
The operator's orders: "no hedging" (10-27); "dont restrict the claim"; "'yang mills in 4d is not claimed' is not a concession … claim it" (19:16Z); "keep going on the crossover"; "then find a method that crosses it" (10-29 07:31Z); "nothing above β = 0.2452 is claimed-why not?" - Hypothesis II (§6):
  - Prop 6.6: for observables of the coarse variables, Theorem 8.5 needs only Hypothesis I. The gap of every limit point then follows from Hypothesis I on all shifted block lattices (translation invariance) with a reflection-covariant, UV-stable block map, and a density statement (D) for the limit. Hypothesis II is one of two possible bridges; (D) is the other.
  - Prop 6.2: the paper's example block map (holonomy along a path) breaks down in the ultraviolet: at the Gaussian level its variance is 4G₃(0)·n, so the law tends to Haar; Hypothesis I becomes trivial and Hypothesis II carries the whole problem. Block averages are stable (H⁻¹).
  - Remark 6.3 corrects the paper: Hypothesis I can hold only past the crossover, so Hypothesis II at a_c includes the window. Thm 6.3 splits Hypothesis II into the UV fibre plus the window fibre.
  - Prop 6.1: Theorem 8.5 uses II(a) only averaged over the blocked measure.
  - Thm 6.4: at the Gaussian level the fibre covariance does not depend on the coarse configuration.
  - Thm 6.5: averaged and L^q forms of Hypothesis II for M2, given (S) (Bałaban's Theorem 1 with inserted observables) and a Peierls bound on large fields.
- (E0), subsequential limits for coarse observables: compactness plus Bałaban's bounds.

## M2 — the crossover in L² (proof agent), work math/M2/WORK.md, reader l2dob.c, records math/M2/rec/

PROVED (per the agent; to be read by a second reader)
- Prop 1.1 (§1): the gap is exactly −log‖P‖ on L²₀(π). The averaged bound (λ₁/λ₀)^{2k} ≤ ∫χ²(Pᵏ(x,·)‖π)dπ equals Z_{2k}/λ₀^{2k} − 1, and Prop 8.1's coefficient dominates it.
- Lemma 2.1 / 7.2 (§2, §7): the L² two-site lemma ‖Q_zK_yF‖ ≤ ‖Q_zF‖ + R‖Q_yF‖ for any two projections (Halmos), R the cosine of their Friedrichs angle, i.e. the conditional maximal correlation.
- Prop 2.2 and Thm 2.3: |Cov(F,G)| ≤ Σ ℓ_x(F)(I−R)⁻¹(x,y)ℓ_y(G), and Var ≤ (1−ρ(R))⁻¹ΣE Var(·|v_{y^c}); weighted row sums < 1 give exponential decay.
- Prop 2.5: ρ² ≤ C(A|B)C(B|A), so Hypothesis I implies I-L².
- Thm 3.1, Theorem 8.5-L² (§3): the gap of every limit point, m = a_c⁻¹min(κ, log 1/ρ′), follows from I-L² plus a fibre clause AVERAGED over the blocked measure — a strengthening of Theorem 8.5, the averaged clause being the form a small-field expansion delivers.
- Thm 4.1 (§4): every such hypothesis refuses U(1) wherever it is massless (rigorously in Fröhlich–Spencer's range; at 1.2 by the measured phase diagram). For the free field ρ(R) = 1 exactly; the massive field passes. The negative half of the group-dependence; the positive half is not derived.
- Lemmas 5.1 / 7.1: the mixture inequality, given ξ ⊂ σ(ω). The fine-data supremum (the record's convicted one) leaves the bound; the direct part enters as E_ν[ρ_ω] times an O(1) density ratio.

COMPUTED (stakes in §6.3 before every run; double precision on two roads — readings, not certificates)
- Known answers met (§6.4): synthetic laws ρ = ε; the record's C reproduced; Roads A and B agree to 3×10⁻⁶; the Schur reconstruction matches.
- Integrity finding (§6.0): the bent engine enters the n+1 corner plaquettes twice as outside staples; every bent number of the record at a named datum is a planar-geometry number, and the record's S₂₀ is not the bent row.
- Two-step adjacent sum 20ρ (20C in brackets): SU(2) 2.2 W2L2 ordered 0.304 (0.526), cancelling 0.349 (0.598); SU(2) 2.2 W3L2 0.630 (1.113), 0.772 (1.329); U(1) 1.2: 2.40 (3.33), 2.96 (3.92); U(1) 0.9 (gapped): 1.31, 1.49. This reading reads coupling at this scale, not the gap. ρ/C in 0.44–0.59 for SU(2), 0.55–0.78 for U(1).
- W3 is not converged: W2 → W3 multiplies the two-step ρ by about 2.1; a further factor above 1.3 at W4 would put the sharper sum above one. The loop cap moves ρ by at most 2.2%.
- Stakes: S1, S3, S4, S6 held; S2 refuted at one cell (0.551); S5's second clause refuted (4.0%, not more than 10%). The U(1) planar run was stopped after 786 CPU-seconds with no output.
- Item (4): BondMoving's bounds admit a constrained weight w·1_B. The sandwich controls the chessboard weight z(B) (an O(1) free-energy density), not Z_{2k}. The bad event must be compound, since z ≥ μ(tr U_p ≤ 0) ≈ 0.1.

NEXT OBJECTS (§8): replace the coarse-environment supremum (it enters only through a Minkowski step) by Hölder along P_tF, controlled by hypercontractivity, bad environments priced at z^{1/2p} (failure mode named first: log-Sobolev constants carry their own supremum); a bent-pair engine integrating the corner plaquettes as cycles; the outside-mediated variance ρ(A;ω)ρ(B;ω); the W4L2 two-step run (4 GB, hours); interval Gram certification; the vortex free-energy bound on the centre-odd (½,½) channel, which carries the two-step coupling (ρ/C → 3π/16).

CROSS-FIELD (to verify against Liu–Wong–Kong 1994 and Amit): Thm 2.3 is the multi-site form of the two-block Gibbs-sampler rate, equal to the maximal correlation; R is the χ²-contraction coefficient, which is why the L² currency tensorises; R is a Friedrichs angle (alternating projections).

Did not cross the window; an exact structural reduction plus readings. PROVED: §2 Theorem 2.1 (twist identity): for THE GROUND's holonomy block map and averaged maps, a coarse centre flip obeys ℓ(σv) = ℓ(v)·ν(B_f|v) — the blocked density's centre ratio is the fibre expectation of a closed 't Hooft surface; F = −log ν(B_f|v), the conditional vortex free energy, independent of the representing surface. Prop. 2.2 (vortex recursion): e^{−F_{k+1}} = E[e^{−F_k}|coarser data], exactly. §3 Lemma 3.1, Cor. 3.1′, Theorem 3.3: R̃ ≤ R ≤ (R̃² + T² + T′²)^{1/2} — Hypothesis I-L² splits into an SO(3) (integer-spin) channel (necessary) and a vortex sensitivity T ≤ 2 sinh Δ (Δ THE GROUND's mixed difference restricted to centre orbits). §4 Theorem 4.1: T = the response of a twisted fibre at distance a_c/2 (explicit constants under a fibre Dobrushin condition) — the vortex half is a fibre statement. §5 Theorem 5.1 (no-go): centre-blind actions have T ≡ 0 and R = R̃ — no vortex criterion bounds the SO(3) half (unconditional at the level of laws; the gauge instance rests on van Enter–Shlosman, conditional on their theorem covering SO(3)). §8 Theorem 8.1 (Turán slaving): on convolution couplings the SO(3) channel ≤ (vortex channel)²; Wilson weights in the class at every β (log-concavity of I_n). COMPUTED (planar geometry, pinned datum, truncated; no bent number used; all known answers met, full ρ = M2's Road A to every digit): at two steps from 2.2 the direct coupling ≥ 99.37 % vortex channel, SO(3) share ≤ 0.87 %; ρ_EE/ρ_OO² = 0.069–0.28; per step the odd channel contracts ×0.14–0.36 (SU(2)) vs ×0.28–0.67 (U(1)), the even ×0.003–0.011 vs ×0.005–0.026 (fixed data, two rungs). Stakes S2, S6, part of S5 REFUTED. THE CRITERION (§1b, §9): route 1's identities lose nothing per step (cannot carry the group difference); the group lives in how fast F contracts per step (one loop: 1 − 0.2575/β_eff for SU(2), 1 for U(1)); admissible step β(k+1) ≤ β(k) − 0.2575 + δ, δ ≥ 0 (zero drift refuses U(1) at every depth); reaching β ≈ 0.45 from 2.2 in 8 steps needs δ < 0.039 per step; the hierarchical comparison loses δ = 0.40 at rung 1 (2.34 vs 1.94); the cone has the same budget. NEXT OBJECT LEMMA W: (W-V) the twisted fibre forgets a 't Hooft insertion at a_c/2 (→ M5); (W-S) the outside-mediated integer-spin covariance S_ξ(A,B) = sup|Cov_ν(E_ω f(Ã), E_ω g(B̃))|, the scalar glueball across one block (→ M2b); first exact test the two-face object with an integrated outside ring, by channel. §12: the two halves = M2b's two global inputs; Theorem 3.3 decomposes M2b's object (3). Unifications: a maximal correlation splits over the dual of the centre; 't Hooft duality exact and conditional with its own exact RG recursion; the SU(2) crossover is the Widom line of the fundamental–adjoint endpoint (MC, not proof), the light 0⁺⁺ centre-even (the half the centre cannot see) — suggests an adjoint-poor RP bare action; one Turán inequality gives THE GROUND's Lemma 8.6 constant and the slaving.

VERDICT: the reduction PROVED; (E2) CONDITIONAL on (E1-scale) (limits at different renormalization conditions one dilation family — a separate input), E2-UV(T), (F2-Lip), Hypotheses I–II at the exit; only the skew step and Lemma Σ_χ new beyond the gap and E1.
